check mark if the registrant has elected not to use the extended transition period for complying with any new or revised financial accounting standards provided pursuant to Section 13(a) of the Exchange Act. &#9744;</span></div><div style="margin-top:3pt;text-align:justify"><table style="border-collapse:collapse;display:inline-table;margin-bottom:5pt;vertical-align:text-bottom;width:99.853%"><tr><td style="width:1.0%"/><td style="width:98.900%"/><td style="width:0.1%"/></tr><tr style="height:9pt"><td colspan="3" style="border-bottom:1pt solid #000000;border-top:1pt solid #000000;padding:0 1pt"/></tr></table></div><div style="text-align:center"><span><br/></span></div><div id="i0240526021c042ca8ff03008326caa92_4"></div><div><span><br/></span></div><div><span><br/></span></div><div><span><br/></span></div><div><span><br/></span></div><div><span><br/></span></div><div style="height:42.75pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:center"><span><br/></span></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"/><div style="min-height:42.75pt;width:100%"><div><span><br/></span></div></div><div style="-sec-extract:summary"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Item 5.02 Departure of Directors or Certain Officers; Election of Directors; Appointment of Certain Officers; Compensatory Arrangements of Certain Officers.</span></div><div><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">On April 7, 2026, the Leadership Development, Inclusion and Compensation Committee (&#8220;LDICC&#8221;) of the Board of Directors of Alphabet Inc. (&#8220;Alphabet&#8221;) approved equity awards for the following executive officers: Anat Ashkenazi, Senior Vice President, Chief Financial Officer, Alphabet and Google LLC (&#8220;Google&#8221;); Ruth Porat, President and Chief Investment Officer, Alphabet and Google; Philipp Schindler, Senior Vice President, Chief Business Officer, Google; and Kent Walker, President, Global Affairs, Chief Legal Officer and Secretary, Alphabet and Google. </span></div><div><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">Alphabet uses a combination of both Alphabet performance stock units (&#8220;PSUs&#8221;) which vest, if at all, based on long-term company performance, and restricted stock units (&#8220;GSUs&#8221;), which provide incentive for continued service.  This approach benefits Alphabet and is designed to maximize long-term shareholder value.  The equity awards, which were granted on April 8, 2026, are as follows:</span></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><span><br/></span></div><div style="padding-left:36pt;text-indent:-18pt"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:14.5pt">Ms. Ashkenazi was granted PSUs with a target value of $10,000,000, and GSUs in the amount of $20,000,000.</span></div><div style="padding-left:36pt;text-indent:-18pt"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:14.5pt">Ms. Porat was granted PSUs with a target value of $9,000,000, and GSUs in the amount of $20,000,000.</span></div><div style="padding-left:36pt;text-indent:-18pt"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:14.5pt">Mr. Schindler was granted PSUs with a target value of $16,000,000, and GSUs in the amount of $26,000,000.</span></div><div style="padding-left:36pt;text-indent:-18pt"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:14.5pt">Mr. Walker was granted PSUs with a target value of $9,000,000, and GSUs in the amount of $20,000,000.</span></div><div><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">The GSU awards also include a transitional amount, on top of the annual target value, to maintain target total compensation following the discontinuation of the SVP Bonus program in 2025. 2026 is the second and final year of the SVP bonus transition.  The transitional amounts were granted on April 8, 2026 as an addition to the 2026 GSU awards. Each individual&#8217;s transitional amount follows: $6,000,000 for Ms. Ashkenazi, $5,000,000 for Ms. Porat and Mr. Walker, and $5,666,667 for Mr. Schindler.</span></div><div><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">The target number of PSUs was calculated by dividing the target value of the total PSU grant by the average closing price of Alphabet&#8217;s Class C capital stock during the month of March 2026 (the &#8220;Average Closing Price&#8221;). The PSUs will depend on Alphabet&#8217;s total shareholder return (relative to S&amp;P 100 companies) (&#8220;relative TSR&#8221;), and this performance-based equity may not vest at all. The vest will be based on the relative TSR performance over a 2026-2028 performance period, subject to continued employment.  Depending upon Alphabet&#8217;s relative TSR performance, the number of PSUs that vest in a tranche will range from 0%-200% of target.  Upon vesting, each PSU will entitle the grantee to receive one share of Alphabet&#8217;s Class C capital stock.</span></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">The number of GSUs was calculated by dividing the GSU award amount by the Average Closing Price. The total GSU award is composed of the (i) target GSU award, which vests monthly over three years from 2026 through 2028, and (ii) a supplemental (transitional) award, which vests monthly during 2026 - other than:</span></div><div><span><br/></span></div><div style="padding-left:36pt;text-indent:-18pt"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:14.5pt">a 4-month catch-up vest in April 2026 for both awards, and</span></div><div style="padding-left:36pt;text-indent:-18pt"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:14.5pt">a vesting date shift for all employees (from the 25th to the 1st) in March 2027 resulting in a cumulative 2-month vest.</span></div><div><span style="font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%"></span></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">All vesting is contingent on the recipient's continued employment on the applicable vesting date(s). Upon vesting, each GSU will entitle the grantee to receive one share of Alphabet&#8217;s Class C capital stock. </span></div><div><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">Upon death, all unvested GSUs will accelerate immediately and all performance-based equity will immediately vest at target (or based on actual performance if the performance period ended prior to death). Upon termination of employment by Alphabet for cause, all unvested equity will be forfeited. Upon termination of employment by Alphabet without cause, the grantee will be eligible to vest in a pro-rata number of all performance-based equity (based on actual performance at the end of the performance period). All Alphabet GSUs and PSUs are subject to the terms and conditions of Alphabet&#8217;s Amended and Restated 2021 Stock Plan and the applicable award agreement.</span></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">The foregoing description is only a summary of the terms of the respective awards and is qualified in its entirety by reference to the full text of the applicable award agreements, which will be filed as exhibits to Alphabet&#8217;s quarterly report on Form 10-Q.</span></div><div><span><br/></span></div><div style="height:42.75pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:center"><span><br/></span></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"/><div style="min-height:42.75pt;width:100%"><div><span><br/></span></div></div><div style="text-align:center"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">SIGNATURE</span></div><div style="margin-bottom:6pt;margin-top:9pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:24.75pt"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">Pursuant to the requirements of the Securities Exchange Act of 1934, the registrant has duly caused this report to be signed on its behalf by the undersigned hereunto duly authorized.</span></div><div style="margin-bottom:6pt;margin-top:9pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:24.75pt"><table
